        <revisionDesc>
            <change when="2020-11-04" who="Dominik Wujastyk">Began this file.</change>
            <change when="2023-11-04" who="Dominik Wujastyk"> added 1.41, based on MS N</change>
            <change when="2023-06-26" who="Dominik Wujastyk">Separated the file into two parts, 1-31
                and 32-end, because of processing limits at Saktumiva</change>
            <change when="2023-10-25" who="Dominik Wujastyk">Added all remaining adhyāyas from K
                (from H for 19 and 20) and stripped out transcription codes to make draft
                provisional edition files.</change>
            <change when="2024-02-05" who="Lisa Brooks">The numbering and text of SS.1.13 are
                collated against the 1938 edition, not the 1931 edition.</change>
            <change when="2024-06-24" who="Deepro Chakraborty">Finalized the edition of
                SS.1.8</change>
            <change when="2024-03-27" who="Dominik Wujastyk">Completed a major overhaul
                ("meta-edit") of the all Sūtrasthāna files. I checked all passages and made sure
                that xml:id tags matched correctly across all files, unifying and separating
                text-portions as needed. I separated words ad hoc. I corrected readings and checked
                against the MSS in many cases, but again ad hoc. There is much remaining to be done,
                but the Sūtrasthāna is now in a coherent format at the macro scale.</change>
            <change when="2025-09-10" who="Deepro Chakraborty">Corrections in 1.12</change>
            <change when="2025-09-16" who="Deepro Chakraborty">Rearranged xml ids, made other
                corrections and added the missing part in 1.12</change>
            <change when="2025-11-02" who="Deepro Chakraborty">Minor corrections in 1.12</change>
            <change when="2025-11-11" who="Deepro Chakraborty">minor corrections in
                1.13.1-12</change>
            <change when="2025-11-13" who="Deepro Chakraborty">minor corrections in 1.13</change>
            <change when="2025-12-02" who="Deepro Chakraborty">splitting 1.13.22</change>
            <change when="2026-01-05" who="Deepro Chakraborty">Removed extra splitting of lines in
                1.12 that was made in order to collate it against L. The file with splitting is sent
                to Dominik Wujastyk through email.</change>
            <change when="2026-03-31" who="Deepro Chakraborty">Added xml:id="SS.1.27.18-19 and
                updated its reading</change>
            <change who="Dominik Wujastyk" when="2026-04-04">Completed the addition of verses
                1.29.add1 - add10 that were skipped in the original transcription of all three
                manuscripts, K, N, and H</change>
        </revisionDesc>

    <text xml:lang="sa-Latn-t-sa-Deva" type="edition">
        <body>

            <div n="1" type="sthāna">

                <head xml:lang="en">[Sūtrasthāna 1-31]</head>

                <div n="1" type="adhyāya">

                    <head>[Adhyāya 1]</head>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.0">
                        <l>
                            <g ref="#newa-siddhi"/> namaḥ kamalahastāya ||</l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.1">
                        <l> athāto vedotpattim adhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.1.1]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.2">
                        <l/>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.3">
                        <l> atha khalu bhagavantam amaravaram ṛṣigaṇaparivṛttam āśramasthaṃ
                            kāśirājaṃ divodāsam<anchor xml:id="SS.1.1.3-n1"/> aupadhenava<pc
                                > </pc>vaitaraṇaurabhra<pc> </pc>puṣkalāvata<pc
                                > </pc>karavīra<pc> </pc>gopurarakṣita<pc> </pc>bhoja<pc
                                > </pc>suśruta<pc> </pc>prabhṛtaya ūcuḥ |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.1.3]</trailer>
                    </lg>




                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.4">
                        <l> bhagavañ śārīramānasāgantubhir vyādhibhir vividhavedanābhighātopadrutān
                            sanāthān anāthavad viceṣṭamānān vikrośataś ca mānavān abhisamīkṣya
                            manasi naḥ pīḍābhavat |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.1.4]</trailer>
                    </lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.4b">
                        <l> teṣāṃ sukhaiṣiṇāṃ rogopaśamārtham ātmanaś ca prāṇayātrārtham āyurvedaṃ
                            icchāma upadiśyamānam | atrāyattam aihikam āmuṣmikañ ca śreyas tad
                            bhagavantam upasannāḥ smaḥ śiṣyatveneti |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.1.4b]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.5">
                        <l> tān uvāca bhagavān svāgatam vaḥ sarva evāmīmāṃsyā adhyāpyāś ca bhavanto
                            vatsāḥ |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.1.5]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.6">
                        <l> iha khalv āyurvedo nāma yad upāṅgam atharvavedasyoktam anutpādyaiva ca
                            prajāḥ ślokaśatasahasram adhyāyasahasrañ ca kṛtavān svayambhūr
                            alpāyuṣkālpamedhastvañ cālokya narāṇām bhūyo 'ṣṭadhā praṇītavān | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.1.6]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.7">
                        <l> tad yathā śalyaṃ śālākyaṃ kāyacikitsā bhūtavidyā kaumārabhṛtyam
                            agadatantraṃ rasāyanatantraṃ vājīkaraṇatantram iti |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.1.7]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.8.1">
                        <l> athāsya pratyekāṅgalakṣaṇasamāsaḥ |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.1.8.1]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.8.1a">
                        <l> tatra śalyan nāma
                            vividhatṛṇakāṣṭhapāṣāṇapāṃsulohaloṣṭāsthibālanakhapūyāsrāvaduṣṭavraṇāntargarbhaśalyoddharaṇārthaṃ
                            yantraśastrakṣārāgnipraṇidhānaviniścayārthañ ca ṣaṣṭyābhidhānair iti | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.1.8.1a]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.8.2">
                        <l> śālākyatantran nāmorddhvajatrugatānāṃ vikārāṇāṃ
                            śravaṇanayanavadanaghrāṇādisaṃśritānāṃ vikārāṇām upaśamakaraṇārtham
                            |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.1.8.2]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.8.3">
                        <l> kāyacikītsā nāma sarvaśarīrāvasthitānāṃ vyādhīnām upaśamakaraṇārthaṃ
                            jvaraśophagulmaraktapittonmādāpasmārapramehātisārādīnāñ ca |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.1.8.3]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.8.4">
                        <l> bhūtavidyā nāma
                            devagandharvayakṣarākṣasapitṛpiśācavināyakanāgagrahopasṛṣṭacetasāṃ
                            śāntikarmabaliharaṇādigrahopaśamanārthaṃ |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.1.8.4]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.8.5">
                        <l> kaumārabhṛtyan nāma kumārabharaṇadhātrīkṣīradoṣasaṃśodhanārthaṃ
                            duṣṭastanyagrahasamutthitānāñ ca vyādhīnām upaśamakaraṇārtham |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.1.8.5]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.8.6">
                        <l> agadatantran nāma sarpakīṭadaṣṭaviṣavyañjanārthaṃ
                            vividhaviṣavegopaśamanārthañ ca |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.1.8.6]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.8.7">
                        <l> rasāyanatantran nāma vayaḥsthāpanam āyurmedhākaraṇaṃ
                            vyādhyupaśamakaraṇārthañ ca |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.1.8.7]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.8.8">
                        <l> vājīkaraṇatantran nāma svalpaduṣṭakṣīṇaviśuṣkaretasāṃ
                            śukrāpyāyanaprasādopacayajanananimittaṃ praharṣajananārthañ ca |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.1.8.8]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.9">
                        <l> evam ayam āyurvedo 'ṣṭāṅga upadiśyate | atra kasmai kiṃ varṇyatām iti
                            |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.1.9]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.10">
                        <l> ta ūcur asmākaṃ sarvam eva śalyajñānam alaṅkṛtvopadiśatu bhagavān iti
                                |<anchor xml:id="SS.1.1.10-n1"/>
                        </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.1.10]</trailer>
                    </lg>



                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.11">
                        <l> sa uvācaivam astv iti |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.1.11]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.12">
                        <l> ta ūcur bhūyo 'smākaṃ sarveṣām evaikamatīnāṃ matam abhisamīkṣya suśruto
                            bhagavantaṃ prakṣyati | asyopadiśyamānaṃ vayam apy upadhārayiṣyāmaḥ
                            |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.1.12]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.13">
                        <l> sa uvācaivam astv iti | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.1.13]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.14-15">
                        <l> iha khalv āyurvedaprayojanaṃ vyādhyupasṛṣṭasya vyādhiparimokṣaḥ
                            svastharakṣaṇañ cāyur asmin vidanty anena vāyur vidyata ity āyurvedaḥ
                            |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.1.14-15]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.16">
                        <l> tasyāṅgavaram āgamapratyakṣānumānopamānair aviruddham ucyamānam
                            upadhārayadhvam | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.1.16]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.17">
                        <l> etad dhy aṅgaṃ prathamaṃ pradhānaṃ prāg abhihitatvād
                            vraṇasaṃrohaṇakaratvād yajñaśiraḥpradhānasandhānāc ca | śrūyate hi yathā
                            purā rudreṇa śiraś chinnam aśvibhyāṃ punaḥ sandhitam iti | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.1.17]</trailer>
                    </lg>



                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.18">
                        <l> aṣṭānām api cāyurvedatantrāṇām etad evādhikam āśu kriyākaraṇād
                            yantraśastrakṣārāgnipraṇidhānāt sarvatantrasāmānyāc ca |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.1.18]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.19">
                        <l> tad idaṃ śāśvataṃ puṇyaṃ svargyaṃ yaśasyam āyuṣyaṃ vṛttikarañ ca | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.1.19]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.20">
                        <l> tad brahmā provāca tat prajāpatir adhijage tasmād aśvināv aśvibhyām
                            indra indrād ahaṃ mayā tv iha pradeyam arthibhyaḥ prajāhitahetoḥ |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.1.20]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.21">
                        <l> bhavati cātra |<anchor xml:id="SS.1.1.21-n1"/>
                            <caesura/>
                             ahaṃ hi dhanvantarir ādidevo jarārujāmṛtyuharo marāṇāṃ |
                            <caesura/>
                             śalyam mahac chāstravaraṃ gṛhītvā prāpto 'smi gāṃ bhūya ihopadeṣṭuṃ | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.1.21]</trailer>
                    </lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.22">
                        <l> tatrāsmiñ śāstre pañcamahābhūtaśarīrisamavāyaḥ puruṣa ity ucyate |
                            tasmin kriyā so 'dhiṣṭhānaṃ | kasmāl | lokadvaividhyāl | loko hi
                                dvividhaḥ<anchor xml:id="SS.1.1.22-n1"/> sthāvaro jaṅgamaś ca |
                            dvividhātmaka evāgneyaḥ saumyaś ca tadbhūyastvāt pañcātmako vā | tatra
                            caturvidho bhūtagrāmaḥ saṃsvedajādrijajarāyujāṇḍajasaṃjñaḥ | tasmin
                            puruṣaḥ pradhānas tasyopakaraṇam anyat | tasmāt puruṣo 'dhiṣṭhānaṃ | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.1.22]</trailer>
                    </lg>





                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.23">
                        <l> tad duḥkhasaṃyogā vyādhaya ity ucyante |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.1.23]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.24">
                        <l> te caturvidhā āgantavaḥ śārīrā mānasā svābhāvikāś ceti | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.1.24]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.25.1">

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.25.2">

                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.1.25.2]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.25.3">

                    </lg>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.25.4">

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.26">

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.27">
                        <l> teṣāṃ lekhanabṛmhaṇasaṃśodhanasaṃśamanāhārācārāḥ samyak prayuktā

                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.1.27]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.28">
                        <l> prāṇināṃ punar mūlam āhāro balavarṇaujasāñ ca | sa ṣaṭsu raseṣv āyattaḥ
                            | rasāḥ punar dravyāśrayinaḥ | dravyāṇi punar oṣadhyaḥ | tā dvividhā |
                            sthāvarā jaṅgamāś ca | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.1.28]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.29">
                        <l> tāsāṃ sthāvarāś caturvidhā vanaspatayo vṛkṣā oṣadhyo vīrudha iti | tāsv
                            apuṣpā phalavantyo vanaspatayaḥ | puṣpaphalo ye tā vṛkṣāḥ |
                            phalapākaniṣṭhās tv oṣadhyaḥ | pratānavatyo vīrudha iti |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.1.29]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.30">
                        <l> jaṅgamāḥ khalv api caturvidhāḥ | jarāyujāṇḍajasaṃsvedajodbhidā iti |
                            teṣām paśumanuṣyavyālādayo jarāyujāḥ | khagasarīsṛpasarpās tv aṇḍajāḥ |
                                kṛmi<pc> </pc>kuntha<pc> </pc>pipīlikā<pc> </pc>prabhṛtayaḥ<anchor
                                xml:id="SS.1.1.30-n1"/> saṃsvedajāḥ | indragopakamaṇḍūkaprabhṛtayaś
                            codbhidāḥ | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.1.30]</trailer>
                    </lg>



                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.31">
                        <l> tatra sthāvarebhyas
                            tvakpatrapuṣpaphalamūlakandakṣīraniryāsasārasnehasvarasāḥ
                            prayojanavantaḥ | jaṅgamebhyaś carmaromanakharudhirādayaḥ | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.1.31]</trailer>

                                > </pc>śaurya<pc> </pc>śaucācāra<pc> </pc>vinaya<pc

                            tasmai deyam ato|| viparītaguṇaṃ nopanayet | </l>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.2.4">

                            śucau deśe gocarmamātraṃ sthaṇḍilam upalipya darbhasaṃstaraṇam ahitaṃ
                            kṛtvā puṣpair dhūpair janvair bhankais ca pūjayitvā
                            palāśodumvarabilvānāṃ samidbhir ghṛtam aktābhir dārvīhaumikenāgnim
                            upasamādhāyājyañ juhuyāt | pratidevatam ṛṣibhyaḥ śiṣyaṃ svāhākāraṃṅ
                            kārayet | </l>

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.2.6">

                                > </pc>pārūṣya<pc> </pc>paiśunyānṛtālasyāsyayaśasyāni hitvā

                            aphalā ca vidyā na ca prākāśyaṃ prāpnuyāt | </l>

                            dravyarasaguṇavīryavipākadoṣadhātumalāśayamarmmasirāsnāyusandhyasthivibhāgāḥ
                            | garbhasaṃbhavadravyasamūhavibhāgāś ca | tathā
                            pranaṣṭaśalyoddharaṇavraṇaviniścayabhagnavikalpāḥ | sādhyayāpy
                            apratyākhyeyatā ca vikārāṇām evam ādayaś ca sahasraśo 'nye viśeṣāḥ | ye
                            cintyamānā vimalavipulabuddher api buddhim ākulīkuryuḥ kiṃ punar
                            alpabuddhes tasmād avaśyam anuvarṇayitavyaṃ || </l>

                        <l> etā dehe viśeṣeṇa tava nityā hi devatāḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             etās tāḥ satataṃ pāntu diśantu ca nirāmayaṃ |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.5.30]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.5.31">
                        <l/>
                    </lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.5.32">
                        <l/>
                    </lg>



                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.5.33">
                        <l> etair vedātmakair mantraiḥ | kṛtyavyādhivināśanaiḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             mayaivaṃ kṛtarakṣastvandīrghamāyuravāpnuhi ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.5.33]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.5.34">
                        <l> tataḥ kṛtarakṣakarmamāturamagāram praveśyācārikam upadiśet</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.5.34]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.5.35">
                        <l> tatastṛtīyehani vimucyaivameva badhnīyānna cainaṃ
                            tvaramāṇoparedyurmokṣayet</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.5.35]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.5.36">
                        <l> dvitīyadivasamokṣaṇād vigrathito vraṇaḥ cirād upasaṃrohatyugrarukta
                            bhavati ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.5.36]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.5.37">
                        <l> ata urdhvaṃ doṣakālabalādīn avekṣya kaṣāyālepanabandhāhārād
                            vidadhyān</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.5.37]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.5.38">
                        <l> na cainaṃ tvaramāraṇaḥ sāntardoṣaṃ rohayet | sa hy
                            alpenāpyapacāreṇābhyantaramutsaṅgaṅkṛtvā bhūyo vikaroti |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.5.38]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.5.39abcd">
                        <l> tasmāt suśuddhaṃ bahirabhyantarataś ca vraṇaṃ saṃrohayet |
                            <caesura/>
                             rūḍhe 'py ajīrṇṇavyāyāmavyavāyādīn vivarjjayet ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.5.39abcd]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.5.39ef">
                        <l/>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.5.40">
                        <l> bhavanti cātra||
                            <caesura/>
                             hemante ca vasante ca śiśire cāpi mokṣayet |
                            <caesura/>
                             tryahā dvyahāc charadgrīṣmavarṣāsv api ca buddhimāṃ |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.5.40]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.5.41">
                        <l> atipātiṣu rogeṣu nekṣetainaṃ vidhiṃ bhiṣak |
                            <caesura/>
                             pradīptād gāravacchīghraṃ tatra kuryātpratikriyāṃ |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.5.41]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.5.42">
                        <l> yā vedanāśastranipātajātā tīvrā śarīre pratanoti jantoḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             ghṛtena sā śāntim upaiti neti ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.5.42]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.5.trailer">
                        <l>hya ||</l>
                    </lg>

                <div n="6" type="adhyāya">


                    <head>[Adhyāya 6]</head>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.1">
                        <l> athāto ṛtucaryāṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ ||1||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.6.1]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.2">
                        <l/>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.3">
                        <l> kālo hi bhagavān svayaṃbhur anādimadhyanidhanaḥ | atra
                            rasavyāpatsampattayo jīvitamaraṇe ca manuṣyāṇām āyatte | sa sūkṣmām api
                            kalān na līyata iti kālaḥ | saṅkalayati kalayati vā bhūtānīti kālaḥ
                            ||2||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.6.3]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.4">
                        <l> tasya samvatsarātmano bhagavān ādityo gativiśeṣeṇa
                                nimeṣakāṣṭhākalāmuhūrttāhorātrapakṣamāsartvayana<pc
                                > </pc>samvatsara<pc> </pc>yuga<pc> </pc>pravibhāgaṃ karoti
                            ||3||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.6.4]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.5">
                        <l> tatra laghvakṣinipātamātro nimeṣaḥ | pañcadaśanimeṣā kāṣṭhā |
                            triṅśatkāṣṭhā kalā | viṅśatikalo muhūrttaḥ kalāyāḥ daśabhāgaś ca |
                            triṃśanmuhūrttam ahorātram | pañcadaśāhorātraḥ pakṣaḥ | sa dvividhaḥ
                            kṛṣṇaḥ śuklaś ca | tau māsaḥ ||4|| </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.6.5]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.6">
                        <l> tatra māghādayo dvādaśa māsāḥ samvatsaraḥ | dvimāsikam ṛtuṅ kṛtvā
                            ṣaḍṛtavo bhavanti | te ca śiśiravasantagrīṣmavarṣāśaraddhemantāḥ | teṣān
                            tapastapasyau śiśiraḥ | madhumādhavau vasantaḥ | śuciśukrau grīṣmaḥ
                            nabhonabhasyau varṣā | iṣorjau śarat | sahassahasyau hemanta iti
                            ||5||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.6.6]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.7">
                        <l> ta ete śītoṣṇavarṣavātalakṣaṇāḥ | candrādityayoḥ kālavibhāgakaratvād
                            ayane dve bhavataḥ | dakṣiṇam uttarañ ca | tayor dakṣiṇam
                            varṣāśaraddhemantāḥ | teṣu bhagavān āpyāyate somaḥ | amlalavaṇamadhurāś
                            ca rasā balavanto bhavanti | uttarottarañ ca sarvaprāṇināṃ balam
                            abhivarddhate | uttaraṃ śiśiravasantagrīṣmāḥ | teṣu bhagavān āpyāyate
                            'rkaḥ | kaṭutiktakaṣāyāś ca rasā balavattarā bhavanti | uttarottarañ ca
                            prāṇinām balam parihīyate ||6||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.6.7]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.8">
                        <l> bhavati cātra ||
                            <caesura/>
                             somaḥ kledayate bhūmiṃ sūryaḥ śoṣayate punaḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             tāv ubhāv api saṃśritya vāyuḥ pālayati prajāḥ ||7||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.6.8]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.9">
                        <l> atha khalv ayane yugapat saṃvatsaro bhavati | te dve ayane varṣaḥ
                            saṃvatsaraḥ parivatsaraḥ iḍāvatsaraḥ vatsara ity evaṃ pañca pañca
                            varṣāṇi || te pañca yugam iti saṃjñāṃ labhante | sa eṣa nimeṣādir
                            yugaparyantaḥ kālaś cakravat parivarttamānaḥ kālacakra ity ucyate ||8|| </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.6.9]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.9.add">
                        <l> evaṃ dakṣiṇāyane rātrir vyākhyātā ||9||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en"></trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.10">
                        <l> iha tu varṣāśaraddhemantavasantagrīṣmaprāvṛṣaḥ ṣaḍṛtavo bhavanti
                            doṣopacayaprakopapraśamanimittam | te tu bhādrapadādyair dvimāsikenaivaṃ
                            vyākhyātāḥ | tad yathā | bhadrapadāśvayujau varṣāḥ |
                            kārttikamārgaśīrṣau śarat | pauṣamāghau hemantaḥ | phālgunacaitrau
                            vasantaḥ | vaiśākhajyeṣṭhau grīṣmaḥ | āṣāḍhaśrāvaṇau prāvṛḍ iti ||10||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.6.10]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.11">
                        <l> tatra varṣāsv auṣadhyas taruṇyo 'lpavīryā āhāratvam upagatā vidahyante |
                            āpaś cāpraśāntāḥ kṣitimalaprāyāḥ | tās tūpayujyamānā nabhasi meghāvatate
                            jalapraklinnāyāṃ bhūmau klinnadehānāṃ dehināṃ śīta<pc
                                > </pc>vāta<pc> </pc>varṣa<pc> </pc>viṣṭambhitāgnīnāṃ vidahyante |
                            vidāhāt pittasañcayam āpādayanti | sa sañcayaḥ śaradi praviralameghe
                            viyaty upaśuṣyati paṅke 'rka<pc> </pc>kiraṇa<pc> </pc>pravilāyitaḥ
                            paittikān vyādhīñ janayati | tā evauṣadhyaḥ kālapariṇāmāt pariṇatavīryā
                            balavatyo hemante bhavanti | āpaś ca praśāntāḥ snigdhā atyarthaṃ gurvyaḥ
                            | tā upayujyamānāḥ mandakiraṇatvād bhānos satuṣāropa<pc
                                > </pc>ṣṭambhita<pc> </pc>dehānāṃ dehinām avidagdhāḥ snehād gauravād
                            upalepitvāc ca śleṣmaṇas sañcayam āpādayanti | sa saṃcayo vasante
                                'rka<pc> </pc>kiraṇa<pc> </pc>pravilāyitaḥ śleṣmikān vyādhīn
                            janayati | tā evauṣadhyau grīṣme niḥsārā rūkṣā atimātralaghvyo bhavanty
                            āpaś ca tā upayujyamānāḥ<anchor xml:id="SS.1.6.11.1-n1"/>
                            sūryapratāpopaśoṣitadehānān dehināṃ raukṣyāl lāghavāc ca vāyoḥ sañcayam
                            āpādayanti | sa sañcayaḥ prāvṛṣi cātyarthañ jalopaklinnāyāṃ bhūmau
                            cātiklinnadehānāṃ dehināṃ śītavātavarṣerito vātikān vyādhīñ janayati |
                            evam eṣāṃ doṣāṇāṃ sañcayaprakopahetur uktaḥ ||11|| </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.6.11]</trailer>
                    </lg>







                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.12">
                        <l>
                            tatra varṣāhemantagrīṣmeṣu sañcitānān
                            doṣāṇāṃ śaradvasantaprāvṛṭsu ca prakupitānāṃ nirharaṇam ||12||<anchor xml:id="SS.1.6.12-n1"/></l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.6.12]</trailer>
                    </lg>




                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.13">
                        <l> tatra paittikānāṃ vyādhīnāṃ hemante vyupaśamaḥ | ślaiṣmikāṇān nidāghe
                            vātikānāṃ śaradi | svabhāvatas tv ete sañcayaprakopopaśamā vyākhyātāḥ
                            ||13||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.6.13]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.14">
                        <l> tatra divasapūrvāhṇe vasantaliṅgaṃ madhyāhne grīṣmasya aparāhṇe
                            prāvṛḍliṅgaṃ pradoṣe vārṣikaṃ śāradam ardharātre pratyuṣasi
                            haimanam upalakṣayet | evam ahorātram api varṣam iva
                            śītoṣṇavarṣadoṣopacayaprakopopaśamair jānīyāt ||14||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.6.14]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.15">
                        <l> tatrāvyāpanneṣv ṛtuṣv avyāpannā oṣadhayo bhavanty āpaś ca | tās
                            tūpayujyamānāḥ prāṇāyurbalavīryaujaskaryo bhavanti ||15||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.6.15]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.16">
                        <l> tāsām punar vyāpado 'dṛṣṭakāritāni śītoṣṇavātavarṣāṇi | khalu viparītāny
                            auṣadhīr vyāpādayanty āpaś ca ||16||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.6.16]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.17">
                        <l> tāsām upayogād vividharogaprādurbhāvo marako vā bhavati ||17||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.6.17]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.18">
                        <l/>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.19">
                        <l> kadācid avyāpanneṣv api kṛtyābhiśāparakṣaḥkrodhād adharmair udhvasyante
                            janapadāḥ | viṣauṣadhipuṣpagandhena vā vāyunopanītena |
                            kāsaśvāsapratiśyāyaśirorogajvarair upatapyante prajāḥ
                            grahanakṣatracaritair vā |
                            śayanāsanayānavāhanamaṇiratnopakaraṇagarhitalakṣaṇaprādurbhāvair vā
                            ||18||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.6.19]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.20">
                        <l> jāto 'tra parityāga<pc> </pc>śānti<pc> </pc>prāyaścitta<pc
                                > </pc>bali<pc> </pc>maṅgala<pc> </pc>japa<pc
                                > </pc>homopahārejyāñjali<pc> </pc>namaskāra<pc> </pc>tapo<pc
                                > </pc>niyama<pc> </pc>dayā<pc> </pc>dāna<pc
                                > </pc>dīkṣābhyupagama<pc> </pc>devatā<pc> </pc>brāhmaṇa<pc
                                > </pc>guru<pc> </pc>parair bhavitavyam evaṃ sādhu bhavati ||19||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.6.20]</trailer>
                    </lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.21">
                        <l/>
                    </lg>

                             svaguṇair api yukteṣu viparīteṣu vā punaḥ |
                            <caesura/>

                <div n="7" type="adhyāya">
                    <head xml:lang="en">[Adhyāya 7, collation]</head>



                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.1">
                        <l> athāto yantravidhim adhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.7.1]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.2">
                        <l/>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.2.add">
                        <l> tvahyaṃ sasandhiśrotaḥ snāyvasthikoṣṭhagataśalyād dharaṇārtham
                            upadiśyate |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.7.2.add]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.3">
                        <l> yantraśatam ekottaram atra hastayantram eva pradhānatamaṃ yantrāṇām
                            avagaccha tadadhīnatvād yantrakarmaṇāṃ |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.7.3]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.4">
                        <l> tatra manaḥ śarīrābādhakarāṇi śalyāni teṣāmāharaṇopāyo yantrāṇi</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.7.4]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.5">
                        <l> tāni ṣaṭprakārāṇi bhavanti | tad tyathā | svastikayantrāṇi |
                            sandamśayantrāṇi | tāḍayantrāṇi | nāḍīyantrāṇi | śalākāyantrāṇi |
                            upayantrāṇi ceti ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.7.5]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.6">
                        <l> tatra caturvimśati svastikayantrāṇi | dve sandamśayantre | dve eva
                            tāḍayayantre || viṃśatirnāḍyaḥ aṣṭāviṃśati śalākāḥ | pañcaviṃśati
                            rupayantrāṇīti |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.7.6]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.7">
                        <l> tāni prāyaśo lohāni bhavanti | tat pratirūpakāṇi vā tad alābhe</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.7.7]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.8">
                        <l> tatra nānāprakārāṇāṃ vyāḍānām mṛgapakṣiṇām mukhair mukhāni yantrāṇāṃ
                            prāyaśaḥ sadṛśāni bhavanti | tasmāt sārūpyād āgamād upadeśād anyatra
                            darśanāt | yuktitaś ca kārayet</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.7.8]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.9">
                        <l> samāhitāni yantrāṇi | kharaślakṣṇamukhāni ca |
                            <caesura/>
                             sudṛḍhāni surūpāṇi | sugrahāṇi ca kārayet ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.7.9]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.10">
                        <l> svastikayantrāṇyaṣṭādaśāṅgulāni |
                            siṃhavyāghratarakṣvṛkṣakadvīpimārjjāraśṛgālamṛgervārukākakaṅkakuraracāsabhāsa
                            śaśaghātyulūkacīrillaśyenagṛddhrotkrosabhṛṅgarājāñjalikakarṇṇāvabhañjananandīmukhamukhāni
                            | masūrākṛtibhiḥ kīlair avabaddhāni | mūleṅkuśavadāvṛttavāraṅgāni |
                            asthividaṣṭaśalyoddharaṇārtham upadiśyante |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.7.10]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.11">
                        <l> sanigrahānugrahau sandaṃśau ṣoḍaśāṅgulau |
                            tvaṅmānsasirāsnāyugataśalyoddharaṇārtham upadiśyete |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.7.11]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.12">
                        <l> tāḍayantre dve |dvādaśāṅgule matsyanālakavadekanāladvike
                            karṇṇanāsāsrotogataśalyoddharaṇārtham |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.7.12]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.13">
                        <l> nāḍīyantrāṇy anekaprakārāṇy anekaprayojanāny anekatomukhāny
                            ubhayatomukhāni | srotogatagalaśalyoddharaṇārthaṃ kriyāsaukaryārtham
                            ācūṣaṇārthaṃ rogadarśanārthañ ca | tāni srotodvārapariṇāhāni
                            yathāyogadīrghāṇi bhavanti | tatra bhagandarārśor
                            budavraṇabastyuttarabastimūtravṛddhidakodaradhūmaniruddhaprakaśasanniruddhagudayantrāṇy
                            alābūśṛṅgayantrāṇi copariṣṭād vakṣyāmaḥ |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.7.13]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.14">
                        <l> śalākāyantrāṇy api nānāprakārāṇi nānāprayojanāni
                            yathāyogapariṇāhapradīrghāṇi bhavanti | teṣāṅ gaṇḍūpadaśarapuṅkha |
                            sarppahanu | baḍiśamukhe dve dve | eṣaṇavyūhanacālanāharaṇārtham
                            upadiśyante | masūradalamātramukhe dve kiñcid ānatāgre
                            srotogataśalyoddharaṇārtham ṣaṭkārppāsakṛtoṣṇīṣṇāṇi | pramārjanakriyāsu
                            | kṣārauṣadhapraṇidhānārthantrīṇi darvyākṛtīni khallamukhāni |
                            jāmbvaṃkuśavadanāny agnikarmāṇi trīṇi triṇi | nāsārbudaharaṇārthamekaṃ
                            kolāsthidalamātraṃ khallatīkṣṇoṣṭhaṃ | añjanārthamekaṃ
                            kalāyaparimaṇḍalamubhayato mukulāgraṃ | mūtramārgaviśuddhyarthamekaṃ
                            mālatīpuṣpavṛntāgrapramāṇaparimaṇḍalamiti ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.7.14]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.15">
                        <l> upayantrāṇy api
                            rajjuveṇikācarmāntarvalkalalatāvastrāṣṭhīlāṣmantakamudgarapāṇipādatalāṅgulijihvādantanakhamukhabālāśmaśākhā
                            ṣṭhīvanapravāhaṇaharṣāmaskārttagatāni kṣārāgnibheṣajāni ceti ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.7.15]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.16">
                        <l> etāni dehe sarvasmiṃ dehasyāvayave tathā
                            <caesura/>
                             sandhau koṣṭhe dhamanyā ca yathāyogaṃ prayojayet ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.7.16]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.17">
                        <l> yantrakarmāṇi tu
                            duṣṭavraṇabandhanavyañjanavarttanabhrāmaṇapīḍanatāḍananivaraṇavivaraṇasīvaṇamārgaśodhanavikarṣaṇāharaṇāñchanonnamanavirecabhañjanonmathanacūṣaṇaiṣaṇadāraṇaṛjūkaraṇaprakṣālanapradhamanapramārjanāni
                            caturviṃśati bhavanti ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.7.17]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.18">
                        <l> svabudhyā vibhajed yantrayantrakarmāṇi buddhimān |
                            <caesura/>
                             asaṅkhyeyavikalpatvāc chalyānām iti niścayaḥ ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.7.18]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.19">
                        <l> tatrātisthūlam asāram atidīrgham atihrasvam agrāhivakraṃ śithilam
                            atyunnataṃ mṛdukīlaṃ mṛdupāśaṃ mṛdumukham iti dvādaśayantradoṣāḥ ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.7.19]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.20">
                        <l> etair doṣer vimuktaṃ tu yantram aṣṭādaśāṃgulaṃ |
                            <caesura/>
                             praśastaṃ bhiṣajā jñeyaṃ taddhi karmasu yojayet ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.7.20]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.21">
                        <l> dṛśyaṃ siṃhamukhādyais tu gūḍhaṃ kaṅkamukhādibhiḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             śalyaṃ svastikayantrais tu nirharet tad bhiṣak chanaiḥ ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.7.21]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.22">
                        <l> vivarttate sādhvavagāhate ca gṛhṇāti gṛhyoddharate ca yasmāt |
                            <caesura/>
                             tasmāt smṛtaṅ kāṅkamukhaṃ pradhānaṃ sthāneṣu sarveṣv avikāri yac ceti
                            || </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.7.22]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.trailer">
                        <l> || ṅ ||</l>
                    </lg>


                </div>

                <div n="8" type="adhyāya">

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.8.1">

                                maṇḍalāgrārdhamaṇḍalāgra<pc> </pc>karapatra<pc
                                > </pc>vṛddhipatra<pc> </pc>nakhaśastra<pc

                                > </pc>vetasapatra<pc> </pc>baḍiśa<pc> </pc>dantaśaṅkveṣaṇya iti
                            |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.8.3]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.8.4">
                        <l> tatra maṇḍalāgram ardhamaṇḍalāgrakarapatrāṇi cchedane lekhane
                            copadiśyante | vṛddhipatra<pc> </pc>nakhaśastra<pc
                            > </pc>mudrikotpalapatrakādhyardhadhārāṇi bhedane cchedane copayujyante
                            | sūcī<pc> </pc>kuṭhārikā<pc> </pc>vrīhimukhārā<pc> </pc>vetasapatrāṇi
                            vedhane | eṣaṇy eṣaṇe | anulomāḥ karīrāḥ śastravṛttāś ca | sūcī
                            baḍiśadantaśaṅkuś cāharaṇe |
                            kuśapatrāṭāmukhaśarārimukhāntarmukhatrikurcakāni visrāvaṇe | sūcī
                            sīvyana ity aṣṭavidhaḥ śastrāṇāṃ karmaṇy upayogo vyākhyātaḥ | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.8.4]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.8.5">
                        <l> teṣāṃ yathāyogaṃ grahaṇam | karmasv eṣa śastragrahaṇasamāsaḥ |
                            vṛddhipatran tu vṛntaphalasādhāraṇe bhāge gṛhṇīyāt | bhedanāny evaṃ
                            sarvāṇi | vṛddhipatravad ardhamaṇḍalāgraṃ kiñcid uttānapāṇinā lekhane
                            bahuśo 'vacārya vṛntāgreṇa visrāvaṇāni | viśeṣeṇa tu
                            bālavṛddhasukumārabhīrunārīṇāṃ rājñāṃ rājamātrāṇāṃ vā trikurcakena
                            visrāvayet | talapracchādita<pc> </pc>vṛntāgram aṅguṣṭhapradeśinībhyāṃ
                            vrīhimukham | kuṭhārikāṃ vāmahastagṛhītapucchāṃ
                            dakṣiṇahastāṅguṣṭhāvaṣṭabdhayā madhyamayāṅgulyā nihanyāt | tatra
                                karapatrārāvetasapatrabaḍiśadanta<pc> </pc>śaṅkveṣaṇīr mūle
                            pradeśinīprayuktam |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.8.5]</trailer>
                    </lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.8.6">
                        <l> mudrikāsadṛśaṃ nakhākāraśastramukhañ caturvedhanaṃ sūkṣma<pc
                            > </pc>ḍorāvabaddhaṃ mudrikāśastraṃ teṣān nāmabhir evākṛtayaḥ prāyeṇa
                            vyākhyātāḥ |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.8.6]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.8.7-8">
                        <l> tatra nakhavardhanaiṣaṇyāv aṣṭāṅgulyau sūcyo vakṣyante | śeṣāṇi tu
                            ṣaḍaṅgulāni tāni sugrahāṇi sudhārāṇi surūpāṇi sulohāni sudhautāni
                            samāñcitamukhāni ceti śastrasampat |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.8.7-8]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.8.10">
                        <l> tatra dhārā bhedanānāṃ māsūrī | lekhanānām ardhamāsūrī | vedhyānāṃ
                            visrāvaṇānāñ ca kaiśikī | chedanānām ardhakaiśikī |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.8.10]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.8.11">
                        <l> baḍiśadantaśaṅku cānatāgre | tīkṣṇakaṇṭakaprathamayavapatramukhī
                            yavapatrā eṣaṇī gaṇḍūpadākāramukhī ceti |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.8.11]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.8.9">
                        <l> tatra vakraṃ kuṇṭhaṃ khaṇḍaṃ kharadhārātisthūlam atyalpam atidīrgham
                            atihrasvam ity aṣṭau śastradoṣāḥ | ato viparītaguṇam ādadyād anyatra
                            karapatrāt | tad dhi kharadhāram asthicchedanārtham |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.8.9]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.8.12">
                        <l/>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.8.13">
                        <l> teṣān niśānī ślakṣṇaśilikā dhārāsampādanārthaṃ śālmalīphalakaṃ ceti || </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.8.13]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.8.14">
                        <l> bhavati cātra ||
                            <caesura/>
                             yadā suniśitaṃ śastraṃ romavāhi<pc> </pc>susaṃsthitam |
                            <caesura/>
                             sugṛhītam pramāṇena tadā śastraṃ nipātayet ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.8.14]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.8.15">

                                > </pc>kuravinda<pc> </pc>jalaukāgni<pc> </pc>nakhapatrāṇi ||</l>

                    </lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.8.17">

                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.8.17]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    </lg>

                            karmapatham upadiśet | bahuśruto py akṛtayogaḥ karmasv ayogyo bhavati
                            |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.9.3]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.9.4">
                        <l> tatra puṣpaphalālāvutrapuservārukaprabhṛtiṣu cchedyaśeṣāṃ darśayet |
                            utkartanāpakarttanāni copadiśet | dṛtibastiprasevakapūrṇeṣu bhedyayogyāṃ
                            | saromṇi carmātate lekhyasya | mṛtapaśumahiṣāsvaśirāsūtpalanāleṣu
                            vedhyasya | ghuṇopahatakāṣṭhaveṇunalanāḍīśuṣkālābumukheṣveṣyasya |
                            panasabimbīphalamajjāmṛtapaśudanteṣvāhāryasya
                            sūkṣmaghanavastrāntamṛducarmāntayoḥ sīvyasya |
                            mṛduvadhramāṃsapesyutpalanāleṣu ca karṇṇasandhibandhanayogyām |
                            pustamayapuruṣāṃgapratyaṅgeṣu bandhanayogyāṃ | ghaṭālāvumukheṣu
                            bastivraṇabastipīḍanayogyāṃ | netrapraṇidhānabastipīḍanayor iti || </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.9.4]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                            <caesura/>

                <div n="10" type="adhyāya">



                    <head xml:lang="en">[Adhyāya 10, collation]</head>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.10.1">
                        <l> athāto viśikhānupraveśanīyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.10.1]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.10.2">
                        <l/>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.10.3">
                        <l> adhigatatantreṇopāsitatantrārthena dṛṣṭakarmaṇā kṛtayogyena
                            śāstrārthannigadatā rājānujñātena vaidyena viśikhācaritavyā |
                            nīcanakharomṇā śucinā śucivastraparihitena chatravatā
                            sopānatkenānuddhataveṣeṇa sumanasā kalyāṇābhivyāhāreṇākuhakena
                            bandhubhūtena bhūtānāṃ sahāyavatā |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.10.3]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.10.4.1">
                        <l> tato dūtanimittaśakunamaṅgalānulomyenātura<pc> </pc>gṛham
                            āgamyopaviśyāturam abhipaśyet spṛśet pṛcchec ca tribhir etair
                            vijñānopāyaiḥ | dīrgham āyuṣo 'lpāyuṣo veditavyāḥ |<anchor
                                xml:id="SS.1.10.4.1-n1"/><anchor xml:id="SS.1.10.4.1-n2"/>
                        </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.10.4]</trailer>
                    </lg>




                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.10.4.2">
                        <l/>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.10.5">
                        <l> tatra dṛṣṭvā yau varṇṇavaikṛticchāyāṃ cāturasya bhiṣagjānīyāt | spṛṣṭvā
                            śītoṣṇādīṃ sparśaviśeṣā viparītāviparītāṃ jvaraśophādīṃ | pṛṣṭvā deśaṃ
                            kālaṃ jātisāmyam ātaṅkam utpatti vedanāsamucchrāyo balābalam agniṃ
                            vātamūtrapurīṣāṇām apravṛttipravṛttiṃ ceti ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.10.5]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.10.7">
                        <l> bhavati cātra ||
                            <caesura/>
                             mithyādṛṣṭyā vikārā hi durākhyātās tathaiva ca |
                            <caesura/>
                             tathā duṣparispṛṣṭāś ca mohayeyuś cikitsakaṃ || </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.10.7]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.10.7.add">
                        <l> tasmāt parīkṣyaḥ satataṃ bhiṣajā siddhimicchatā |
                            <caesura/>
                             yuktito vyādhayaḥ sarve pramāṇair darśanādibhiḥ ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.10.7.add]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.10.6">
                        <l> evam abhisamīkṣya sādhyāṃ sādhayed yāpyāṃ yāpayed asādhyān nopakrāmet |
                            parivatsaroṣitāṃś ca vikārām prāyaśaḥ parivarjjayet |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.10.6]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.10.8">
                        <l> tatra sādhyā api vyādhayaḥ prāyaśo duścikitsā bhavanti |
                                śrotriyanṛpatistrībālavṛddhabhīru<pc> </pc>durbala<pc
                                > </pc>vaidyavidagdha<pc> </pc>vyādhiguhaka<pc
                                > </pc>daridra<pc> </pc>kṛpaṇa<pc> </pc>krodhanānātmavatāt ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.10.8]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.10.9">
                        <l> bhavanti cātra ||
                            <caesura/>
                             strībhiḥ sahāsyaṃ saṃvāsa parihāsañ ca varjayet |
                            <caesura/>
                             dattaṃ tābhir na gṛhṇīyād annād anyad bhiṣa deti || </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.10.9]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.10.9.add">
                        <l> vedotpattiśiṣyadīkṣādānam adhyayanasya ca |
                            <caesura/>
                             prabhāṣaṇañ cāgraharaṃ ṛtucaryā tathaiva ca |
                            <caesura/>
                             yantraṃ śastrāvacārañ ca yogyāsūtrīyam eva ca |
                            <caesura/>
                             viśikhānupraveśañ ca proktaṃ vai prathamo daśa || </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.10.9.add]</trailer>
                    </lg>



                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.10.trailer">
                        <l>|| 10 || </l>
                    </lg>

                <div n="11" type="adhyāya">


                    <head xml:lang="en">[Adhyāya 11, collation]</head>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.12.24">
                        <l> grāmyānūpaiḥ sajalajaiḥ piṣṭair māṃsaiś ca lepayet |
                            <caesura/>
                             pittavidradhivac cainaṃ praśāntyoṣmāṇam ācaret |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.12.24]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.12.25">
                        <l> atidagdhe tu śīrṇṇāni māṃsāny uddhṛtya śītalāṃ
                            <caesura/>
                             kriyāṅ kuryāc cūrṇṇakāle śālitaṇḍulakaṇḍanaiḥ |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.12.25]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.12.26">
                        <l> tindukyās tvakkaṣāyair vā mṛdubhṛṣṭair upācaret |
                            <caesura/>
                             vraṇaṅ guḍūcīpatrair vā cchādayed atha codakaiḥ |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.12.26]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.12.27">
                        <l> kriyāṅ kuryāc ca nikhilāṃ bhiṣak pittavisarpavat ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.12.27]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.12.28">
                        <l/>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.12.29ab">
                        <l/>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.12.29cd">
                        <l> athedānīṃ dhūmopahatavidhānaṃ vakṣyāmaḥ |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.12.29cd]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.12.30">
                        <l> śvasity ādhmāti cātyarthaṃ kāsate kṣavate bhṛśaṃ |
                            <caesura/>
                             cakṣuṣaḥ paridāhaś ca rāgaś caivopajāyate |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.12.30]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.12.31">
                        <l> sadhūmakaṃ niśvasiti ghreyam anyan na vetti ca |
                            <caesura/>
                             tathaiva ca rasāṃ sarvāṃ smṛtiś cāsopahanyate |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.12.31]</trailer>
                    </lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.12.32">
                        <l> tṛṣṇādāhajvaraś cetaḥ sīdaty arthañ ca mūrcchati |
                            <caesura/>
                             dhūmopahata ity eṣa śṛṇu tasya cikitsitaṃ ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.12.32abc]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.12.33">
                        <l> sarpir ikṣuraso drākṣā payo vā śarkarāmbu vā |
                            <caesura/>
                             madhurāmlān rasāṃś cāpi vamanārthāya dāpayet || </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.12.33]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.12.34">
                        <l> vamataḥ sudhyate koṣṭhaṃ dhūmagandhas ca naśyati |
                            <caesura/>
                             anena vidhinā tasya kāsasvāsau praśasyataḥ || </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.12.34]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.12.35">
                        <l> ātmānañ ca jvaraś caiva tṛṣṇā dāhas tathaiva ca |
                            <caesura/>
                             madhurair lavaṇāmlaiś ca kaṭukaiḥ kavaḍagrahaiḥ || </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.12.35]</trailer>
                    </lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.12.35ef">
                        <l>vāntasya kaṇṭhaśuddhiḥ syād dhūmagandhaś ca naśyati | </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.12.36ab">
                        <l> samyag gṛhṇātī viṣayān manaś cātra prasīdati ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.12.36ab]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.12.36cd">
                        <l> śirovirecanaṃ cāsmai dadyād yogena śāstravit | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.12.36cd]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.12.37">
                        <l> tenāsya śudhyate dṛṣṭiḥ siraś caivāsya dehinaḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             avidāhi laghu snigdham āhāraṃ cāsya kalpayet | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.12.37]</trailer>
                    </lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.12.38">
                        <l> uṣṇavātātapair dagdhe śītaḥ kāryo vidhiḥ sadā ||
                            <caesura/>
                             śītavarṣānilahate uṣṇaḥ snigdhaś ca śasyate || </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.12.38]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    </lg>

                <div n="13" type="adhyāya">


                    <head xml:lang="en">[Adhyāya 13]</head>

                    <note xml:lang="en">[NB: Based on H alone up to v. 22 and collated against the
                        1938 edition, not the 1931 one.]</note>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.1">
                        <l> athāto jalāyukādhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[1 (1938 ed. 1.13.1)]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.2">
                        <l/>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.3">
                        <l> nṛpāḍhya<pc> </pc>sukumāra<pc> </pc>bāla<pc> </pc>sthavira<pc
                                > </pc>bhīru<pc> </pc>nārīṇām anugrahārthaṃ paramasukumāro 'yaṃ
                            śoṇitāvasecanopāyo 'bhihito jalaukasaḥ || </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[2 (1938 ed. 1.13.3)]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.4">
                        <l> tatra vātapittakaphaduṣṭaśoṇitaṃ yathāsaṃkhyaṃ śṛṅgajalaukālābubhir
                            avasecayet | sarvāṇi sarvair vā visrāvyaṃ ||<anchor
                                xml:id="SS.1.13.4-n1"/>
                        </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[3 (1938 ed. 1.13.4)]</trailer>
                    </lg>






                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.5">
                        <l> bhavanti cātra ||
                            <caesura/>
                             snigdhaṃ ślakṣṇaṃ samadhuraṃ gavāṃ śṛṅgaṃ prakīrttitaṃ |
                            <caesura/>
                             tasmād vātopasṛṣṭe tu hitan tad avasecane || </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[4 (1938 ed. 1.13.5)]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.5.1">
                        <l> arddhacandrākṛti mahattanu saptāṅgulāyataṃ |
                            <caesura/>
                             pracchite dāpayet pūrvvam āsyenācūṣayed balī || <anchor
                                xml:id="SS.1.13.5.1-n1"/>
                        </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[5 ]</trailer>
                    </lg>



                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.6">
                        <l> śītādhivāsā madhurā jalaukā vārisaṃbhavā |
                            <caesura/>
                             tasmāt pittopasṛṣṭe tu hitās tā avasecane || </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[6 (1938 ed. 1.13.6)]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.7">
                        <l> kaṭu rūkṣañ ca tīkṣṇañ ca alābu parikīrttitaṃ |<anchor
                                xml:id="SS.1.13.7-n1"/>
                            <caesura/>
                             tasmāc chleṣmopasṛṣṭe tu hitan tad avasecane || </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.13.7]</trailer>
                    </lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.8">
                        <l> tatra pracchite tanu<pc> </pc>basti<pc> </pc>paṭalāvanaddhena śṛṅgeṇa
                            śoṇitam avasecayet | ācūṣaṇād antarddīptenālābunā | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.13.8]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.9">
                        <l> jalam āsām āyur ity ato jalāyukāḥ | oko nivāso jalam āsām oka ity ato
                            jalaukasaḥ ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.13.9]</trailer>
                    </lg>




                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.10">
                        <l> tā dvādaśa | saviṣāḥ ṣaṭ | tāvantya eva nirviṣāḥ | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.13.10]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.11">
                        <l> kṛṣṇā karburā alagardā indrāyudhā sāmudrikā govandanā ceti | tāsv
                            añjanavarṇṇā pṛthuśīrṣā kṛṣṇā nāma | varmimatsyavad āyatā
                            cchinnonnatakukṣiḥ karburā nāma | romaśā mahāpārśvā kṛṣṇamukhā alagardā
                            nāma | indrāyudhavad ūrddhvarājī citrā indrāyudhā nāma | īṣadasitapītikā
                            vicitrapuṣpākṛticitā sāmudrikā nāma | govṛṣaṇavad adhobhāge
                            dvidhābhūtākṛtir aṇumukhī govandanā nāma | tābhir daṣṭe daṃśe śvayathur
                            atimātraṃ kaṇḍūmūrcchā jvaro dāhaś charddir iti liṅgāni bhavanti | tatra
                            mahāgadaḥ pānālepanādiṣūpayojyaḥ | indrāyudhādaṣṭam asādhyam ity etāḥ
                            saviṣāḥ sacikitsitā vyākhyātāḥ || </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.13.11]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.12">
                        <l> atha nirviṣāḥ | kapilā piṅgalā śaṅkumukhī mūṣikā puṇḍarīkamukhī sāvarikā
                            ceti | tatra manaḥśilārañjitābhyām iva pārśvābhyāṃ pṛṣṭhe
                            snigdhamudgavarṇṇā kapilā nāma | kiñcidraktā vṛttakāyā piṅgalyāśugā
                            piṅgalā nāma | yakṛdvarṇṇā śīghrapāyinī dīrghamukhī śaṅkumukhī nāma |
                            mūṣikākṛtivarṇṇāniṣṭagandhā mūṣikā nāma | mudgavarṇṇā
                            puṇḍarīkatulyavaktrā puṇḍarīkā nāma |
                            padmapatravarṇṇāṣṭādaśāṅgulapramāṇā sāvarikā nāma | sā paśvarthe tv
                            aviṣā vyākhyātāḥ || </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.13.12]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.13">
                        <l> tāsāṃ yavanapāṇḍyasahya<pc> </pc>potanādīni kṣetrāṇi bhavanti | tāsāṃ
                            mahāśarīrā balavatyaḥ śīghrapāyinyo mahāśanā nirviṣāś ca viśeṣeṇa
                            bhavanti | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.13.13]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.14">
                        <l> tatra saviṣakīṭadardduramūtrapurīṣakothajātāḥ kaluṣeṣv ambhaḥsu ca
                            saviṣāḥ | padmotpala<pc> </pc>kumuda<pc> </pc>saugandhika<pc
                                > </pc>śaivāla<pc> </pc>kotha<pc> </pc>jātā vimaleṣv ambhaḥsu ca
                            nirvviṣāḥ || </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.13.14]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.15">
                        <l> bhavati cātra ||
                            <caesura/>
                             kṣetreṣu vicaranty etāḥ salilāḍhyasugandhiṣu |
                            <caesura/>
                             na ca saṅkīrṇṇacāriṇyo na ca paṅkeśayāḥ smṛtāḥ || </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.13.15]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.16">
                        <l> tāsāṃ grahaṇam ārdracarmaṇānyair vā prayogair gṛhṇīyāt<anchor
                                xml:id="SS.1.13.16-n1"/> |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.13.16]</trailer>
                    </lg>



                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.17">
                        <l> athaitā nave mahati ghaṭe sarastaḍākodakapaṅkān āvāpya nidadhyāt |
                            bhaktārthañ cāsām upaharet | śevālaṃ vallūram odakāṃś ca kandāṃś
                            cūrṇīkṛtya śayyārthe tṛṇam odakāni patrāṇi tryahāt tryahāc cāsāṃ
                            jalabhaktan dadyāt | saptarātrāt saptarātrād ghaṭam anyaṃ saṅkrāmayet || </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.13.17]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.18">
                        <l> bhavati cātra |
                            <caesura/>
                             sthūlamadhyāḥ parikliṣṭās tanvyaś cākṣetrajāś ca yāḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             agrāhiṇyo 'lpapāyinyaḥ saviṣāś ca na poṣayet ||<anchor
                                xml:id="SS.1.13.18-n1"/></l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.13.18]</trailer>
                    </lg>



                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.19">
                        <l> atha jalaukāvasekasādhyavyādhiṃ vyādhitam upaveśya saṃveśya vā virūkṣya
                            tam avakāśaṃ mṛdgomayacūrṇṇair yad yat sarujaṃ syād | atha jalaukasaḥ
                                sarṣaparajanī<pc> </pc>pradigdha<pc> </pc>gātryaḥ
                            salilasarakamadhyasañcāriṇī vigatamalāḥ kṛtvā rogaṅ grāhayet |<anchor
                                xml:id="SS.1.13.19-n1"/> atha na gṛhṇatyāḥ kṣīrabinduṃ śoṇitabinduṃ
                            vā nidadhyāt | śastrapadāni vā kurvīta | athaivam api na gṛhṇīyāt anyāṅ
                            grāhayet |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.13.19]</trailer>
                    </lg>



                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.20">
                        <l> yadā niviśate 'śvakhuravad ānanaṅ kṛtvonnāmya ca skandham evañ jānīyād
                            gṛhṇātīti | athainām ārdraplotāvacchannāṅ kṛtvā dhārayet || </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.13.20]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.21">
                        <l> atha daṃśe toda<pc> </pc>kaṇḍū<pc> </pc>prādur<pc> </pc>bhāvāj jānīyāc
                            chuddham ādadātīti | tām apanayet | atha śoṇitagandhena na muñcet |
                            mukham asyāḥ saindhavacūrṇenāvakiret | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.13.21]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.22">
                        <l> athaināṃ śāli<pc> </pc>taṇḍula<pc> </pc>kāṇḍana<pc> </pc>praliptān
                                taila<pc> </pc>lavaṇābhyaktamukhīṃ vāmahastagṛhītapucchān
                            dakṣiṇahastāṅguṣṭhāṅgulībhyāṃ śanaiḥ śanair anulomamārjjayann ā mukhād
                            vāmayed yāvat samyagvānteti | samyagvāntā salilasarake nyastā bhoktukāmā
                            satī cared | yā sīdati na ceṣṭate sā durvāntā punaḥ samyag vāmayet |
                            durvāntāyās tu indramado nāma vyādhir asādhyo bhavati | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.13.22]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.22.1">
                        <l> aprahṛṣṭaśiraḥ pāti kāyenodveṣṭate sakṛt |
                            <caesura/>

                                xml:id="SS.1.13.22.1-n1"/></l>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.22b">

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.23">
                        <l> śoṇitasya ca yogāyogam avekṣya jalaukāmukhaṃ madhunāvaghaṭṭayet |

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.23.1">

                <div n="14" type="adhyāya">


                    <head xml:lang="en">[Adhyāya 14]</head>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.1">
                        <l> athātaḥ śoṇitavarṇṇanīyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.14.1]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.2">
                        <l/>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.3">
                        <l> pāñcabhautikasya caturvidhasyāhārasya ṣaḍrasopetasya
                            dvividhavīryasyāṣṭavidhavīryasya vā anekaguṇopayuktasyāhārasya
                            samyakpariṇatasya yas tejoguṇabhūtaḥ sāraḥ paramasūkṣmaḥ sa rasa ity
                            ucyate | tasya hṛdayaṃ sthānaṃ | sa hṛdayāc caturviṃśatir dhamanīr
                            anupraviśyordhvagā daśa daśa cādhogāminyaś catasras tiryaggāḥ kṛtsnaṃ
                            śarīram aharahas tarpayati jīvayati yāpayati vardhayati cādṛṣṭahaitukena
                            karmaṇā | tasya śarīram anusarato 'numānād gatir upalakṣayitavyā
                            kṣayavṛddhihetukī || tasmiṃ sarvaśarīrāvayavadoṣadhātumalāśayānusāriṇi
                            rase jijñāsā kim ayaṃ saumyas taijasa iti | sa khalu dravatvād anusaraṇe
                            snehanajīvanatarpaṇadhāraṇādibhir viśeṣaiḥ saumya ity avagamyate |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.14.3]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.4">
                        <l> sa khalv āpyo raso yakṛtplīhānau prāpya rāgam upaiti || </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.14.4]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.5">
                        <l> bhavanti cātra ||
                            <caesura/>
                             rañjitās tejasā tv āpaḥ śarīrasthena dehināṃ |
                            <caesura/>
                             avyāpannāḥ prasannena raktam ity eva tad viduḥ || </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.14.5]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.6">
                        <l> rasād eva striyāṃ raktam ṛtusaṃjñaṃ pravartate
                            <caesura/>
                             dvādaśād vardhate varṣād yāti pañcāśataḥ kṣayaṃ |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.14.6]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.7">
                        <l> ārttavaṃ śoṇitaṃ tv āgneyam āhuḥ || agnīṣomīyatvād garbhasya | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.14.7]</trailer>
                    </lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.8">

                            bhavati | evam bālānām api vayaḥpariṇāmāc chukraprādurbhāvo bhavati |

                            anārdrair vraṇamukham avacūrṇṇyāṅgulyagreṇāvapīḍayet |

                            kṣaumeṇa vadhyāsitena samudraphenena lākṣācūrṇṇair vā yathoktair

                            vopacaredagninā vā dahed yathoktaṃ kākolyādikvāthaṃ vā

                            alpavāktvamalpapraharṣo mūḍhasañjñatā ca | pittakṣaye

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.9">
                        <l> rasakṣaye hṛdayapīḍā<pc> </pc>kampaḥ śoṣaḥ śūnyatā tṛṣṇā ca |
                            śoṇitakṣaye tvakpāruṣyam amlaśītaprārthanā sirāśaithilyañ ca ||
                            māṃsakṣaye sphiggaṇṇḍauṣṭhopasthoruvakṣaḥ<pc> </pc>kakṣaśuṣkatā
                            dhamanīnāñ ca śaithilyaṃ | medakṣaye plīhābhivṛddhiḥ sandhiśūnyatā
                            raukṣyaṃ meduramāṃsaprārthanā ca | asthikṣaye asthiśūlo
                            dantanakhabhaṃgāraukṣyañ ca | majjākṣaye lpaśukratā parvabhedo sthitodaḥ
                            śūnyatā | śukrakṣaye meḍhravṛṣaṇavedanāśaktir maithune cirād vā prasekaḥ
                            praseke cālpaśukraraktadarśanaṃ |</l>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.11">
                        <l> purīṣakṣaye hṛdayapārśvapīḍā saśabdasya ca vāyor ūdhvagamanaṃ kukṣau
                            sañcaraṇaṃ ca mūtrakṣaye bastitodo lpamūtratā ca || atrāpi
                            svayonivardhanadravyopayogaḥ || svedakṣaye stabdharomakūpatā
                            sparśavaiguṇyaś ca tatrābhyaṅga svedopayogaś ca ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.15.11]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.12">
                        <l> ārttavakṣaye yathocitakālādarśanamalpatā vā yonivedanā ca | tatra
                            saṃśodhanamāgneyānāñ ca dravyānām upayogaḥ || stanyakṣaye stanayo
                            mlānatā stanyāsambhavaś ca | tatra śleṣmavardhanadravyopayogaḥ ||
                            garbhakṣaye garbhāspandanamanunnatakukṣitā ca | tatra prāptabastikālāyāḥ
                            kṣīrabastiprayogo medhyān na prayogaś ca ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.15.12]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.13">
                        <l> ata ūrdhvam atipravṛddhānāṃ doṣadhātūnāṃ lakṣaṇam upadekṣyāmaḥ tatra
                            vātavṛddhau kārśyaṃ kārṣṇyaṃ gātrasphuraṇatā uṣṇakāmatā
                            nidrānāśolpabalatvaṃ gāḍhavarcasvatā ca || pittavṛddhau pītāvabhāsatā
                            santāpaḥśītakāmitvamalpanidratā mūrcchā balahāniḥ pītaviṇmūtratvañ ca ||
                            śleṣmavṛddhau śauklyaṃ śaithyaṃ sthairyaṃ gauravam agnisādas tandrā
                            nidrā sandhyati śliṣṭatā ca ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.15.13]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.14">
                        <l> raso 'tipravṛddho hṛdaye kledaṃ prasekañ cāpādayati | raktaṃ
                            raktāṅgākṣitāṃ || māṃsaṃ sphiggaṇḍauṣṭhopasthorubāhujaṅghāsu vṛddhiṃ
                            gurugātratāñ ca | medaḥ snigdhāṅgatāmudarapārśvavṛddhiṃ kāsaśvāso
                            daurgandhyañ ca || asthyadhyasthīny adhidantāṃś ca || majjā
                            sarvāṅganetragauravaṃ || śukraṃ śukrāśmariti prādurbhāvaṃ ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.15.14]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.15">
                        <l> purīśam āṭopaḥ kukṣau śūlañ ca || mūtraṃ muhurmuhuḥ pravṛttiṃ todañ ca
                            || svedaḥ kaṇḍū daurgandhyañ ca ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.15.15]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.16">
                        <l> stanyaṃ stanayor atipīnatvaṃ muhurmuhuḥ pravṛttim atitodañ ca ||
                            ārttavamaṅgamardo daurbalyañ ca</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.15.16]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.17">
                        <l> teṣāṃ kṣapaṇam aviruddhaiḥ kriyāviśeṣaiḥ kurvīta |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.15.17]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.18">
                        <l/>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.19">
                        <l> ata ūrdhvam anuvyākhyāsyāmaḥ || rasādīnāṃ śukrāntānāṃ dhātūnāṃ yat paran
                            tejas tat khalv ojas tad eva balam ity ucyate | śāstrasiddhāntāt</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.15.19]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.20">
                        <l> tatra balena sthiropacitamāṃsatā sarvaceṣṭāsvapratighātaḥ
                            svaravarṇṇaprasādo bāhyābhyantarāṇāṃ ca karaṇānām ātmakāryapratipattir
                            bhavati ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.15.20]</trailer>

                            <caesura/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.22ab">

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.24">

                            sadanaṃ doṣacyavanakriyāsannirodhaś ca visraṃse | stabdhatā gurugātra tā
                            śopho varṇṇabhedo glānis tandrā nidrā vyāpanne | māṃsakṣayo mohaḥ

                            <caesura/>
