                <div n="27" type="adhyāya">
                    <head xml:lang="en">[Adhyāya 27, collation]</head>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.1">
                        <l> athātaḥ śalyāpanayanīyam adhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.27.1]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.2">
                        <l/>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.3">
                        <l/>
                    </lg>
                    <!--           <label xml:lang="en">cf. 1938 ed. 1.27.3</label>
                        <caesura/>
                         śalyaṃ dvividham avabaddham anavabaddhaṃ ca || </l>-->

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.4">
                        <l> tatra samāsenānavabaddhaśalyoddharaṇārthaṃ pañcadaśa hetūn vakṣyāmaḥ |
                            tad yathā svabhāvaḥ pācanaṃ bhedanaṃ dāraṇaṃ pīḍanaṃ pramārjanaṃ
                            nirdhmāpanaṃ vamanaṃ virecanaṃ prakṣālanaṃ pratimarśaḥ pravāhaṇaṃ
                            ācūṣaṇaṃ ayaskānto harṣaś ceti || </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.27.4]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.5">
                        <l> tatrāśrukṣavathūdgārakāsamūtrapurīṣānilaiḥ svabhāvabalapravṛttair
                            nayanādibhyaḥ patati māṃsāvagāḍhaṃ śalyamavidahyamānaṃ pācayitvā
                            prakothā(ā.pā)ttasya pūyaśoṇitavegād gauravād vā patati |
                            pakvamabhidyamānaṃ bhedayed dārayed vā | bhinnamanirasyamānaṃ pīḍanīyaiḥ
                            pīḍayet pāṇibhir vā | aṇūny akṣaśalyāni pariṣecanādhmāpanair
                            bālavastrapāṇibhiḥ pramārjayet | āhāraśeṣaśleṣmahīnāṇuśalyāni
                            śvasanotkāsanapradhamanair nirdhamet | annaśalyāni
                            vamanāṅgulipratimarśaprabhṛtibhiḥ | virecanaiḥ pakvāśayagatāni |
                            vraṇadoṣāśayagatāni prakṣālanaiḥ | vātamūtrapurīṣagarbhasaṅgeṣu
                            pravāhaṇamuktaṃ | mārutodakasaviṣarudhiraduṣṭastanyeṣv ācūṣaṇam āsyena
                            viṣāṇair vā | anulomam anavabaddham akarṇam analpavraṇamukham
                            ayaskāntena | hṛdy avasthitam anekakāraṇotpannaṃ śokaśalyaṃ harṣeṇeti || </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.27.5]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.6">
                        <l> sarvaśalyānāṃ tu mahatām aṇūnāṃ vā dvāv evāharaṇahetū bhavataḥ pratilomo
                            'nulomaś ca || </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.27.6]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.7">
                        <l> tatra pratilomamarvācīnamānayet anulomaṃ parācīnam ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.27.7]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.8">
                        <l> uttuṇḍitaṃ chitvā nirghātayecchedanīyamukham || </l>

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.11">

                            <caesura/>
                             saṃrakṣedasya marmāṇi muhur āśvāsayec ca tam || </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.27.11]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.12">
                        <l> tataḥ śalyam uddhṛtya nirlohitaṃ vraṇaṃ kṛtvā svedārham
                            agnighṛtaprabhṛtibhiḥ saṃsvedya vidahya pradihya sarpirmadhubhyāṃ
                            baddhvā+ācārikam upadiśet | (? sirāsnāyuvilagnaṃ śalākādibhir
                            vimocyāpanayet śvayathugrastavāraṅgaṃ samavapīḍya śvayathuṃ
                            durbalavāraṅgaṃ kuśādibhir baddhvā |) </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.27.12]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.13">
                        <l> hṛdayamabhito vartamānaṃ śalyaṃ śītajalādibhir udvejitasyāpahared
                            yathāmārgaṃ durupaharamanyato 'pabādhyamānaṃ pāṭayitvoddharet || </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.27.13]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.14-16">

                            evam aśakyaṃ guṇena pariveṣṭya badhvāśvavaktrakaṭake | pañcāṅgyāṃ
                            vṛkṣaśākhāyām vā acchedyadeśottuṇḍitam aṣṭhīlāsmam udgarāṇām anyatamasya
                            prahāreṇa vicālya yathāmārggata eva yantreṇa mṛditakarṇṇam akarṇṇañ ceti
                            | anābādhakaradeśottuṇḍitāni purastād eva | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.27.14-16]</trailer>
                    </lg>



                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.17">

                            śītābhir adbhiḥ pariṣicya sthirībhūtām śalyam uddharet || </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.27.17]</trailer>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.18-19">

                            dravabhaktopahitaṃ pāyayed ākaṇṭhāt pūrṇakoṣṭhaṃ ca vāmayet vamataś ca
                            śalyaikadeśasaktaṃ jñātvā sūtraṃ sahasā tv ākṣipet mṛdunā vā
                            dantadhāvanakūrcakenāpaharet praṇuded vāntaḥ | kṣatakaṇṭhāya ca
                            madhusarpiṣī leḍhuṃ prayacchet triphalācūrṇaṃ vā madhuśarkarāvimiśram
                            ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.27.18-19]</trailer>

                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.27.20]</trailer>

    </text>
    <standOff corresp="#SS.1.1.3" type="Notes">
        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.1.1.3-n1">The scribe of the earliest MS, K, added the name
                <foreign>dhanvantari</foreign> in the margin; the scribe of N did not have the name;
            the scribe of H included it in the text.</note>
    </standOff>
    <standOff corresp="#SS.1.1.10" type="Notes">
        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.1.1.10-n1">We follow the reading of MSS H and N, pace
            Harimoto 2013, in spite of the fact that <foreign>alaṃkṛ[tvā]</foreign> is a marginal
            addition in K. K is badly damaged at this point, so it is not completely clear what
            happend to the manuscript, although it does look as if the word was not included in the
            text and was added by a different hand. But H is usually a faithful witness to the same
            text as K, and in this case even N concurs. The use of <foreign>alaṅkṛtvā</foreign> in
            the sense "make accessible" is slightly unusual and probably explains the later
            banalization to <foreign>mūla</foreign>. </note>
    </standOff>
    <standOff corresp="#SS.1.1.21" type="Notes">
        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.1.1.21-n1">The Nepalese witnesses commonly use the
            abbreviation "<foreign>bha</foreign>" for "<foreign>bhavati cātra</foreign>",
            introducing the next verse.</note>
    </standOff>
    <standOff corresp="#SS.1.1.22" type="Emendation">
        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.1.1.22-n1">The reading of <foreign>dvividhan</foreign> might
            be a corruption of <foreign>dvividhas</foreign>, which is a permitted sandhi.</note>
    </standOff>
    <standOff corresp="#SS.1.1.30" type="Notes">
        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.1.1.30-n1"><foreign>kuṭṭha</foreign> is Prākṛta for "skin
            disease, leprosy," but this sense does not fit this passage; the word here must refer to
            an insect or similar creature. In our MSS, <foreign>kuṭṭha</foreign> appears to be a
            transmitted form, possibly Sanskritized to <foreign>kuṣṭha</foreign> by the scribe of MS
            H. It is not obvious how it could be a scribal error for the Vulgate's
                "<foreign>kīṭa</foreign>." We read with K.</note>
    </standOff>
    <standOff corresp="#SS.1.1.37" type="Note">
        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.1.1.37-n1"> Note the good example of the inconsistent use by
            the scribe of witness H of anusvāra in 37ab (<foreign xml:lang="sa">°n tu</foreign>) vs
            37cd (<foreign xml:lang="sa">°ṃ tu</foreign>).</note>
    </standOff>
    <standOff corresp="#SS.1.2.3" type="Notes">
        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.1.2.3-n1"> Emending <foreign xml:lang="sa">anvaya</foreign>
            against the Nepalese MSS is required to make syntactic sense of the passage. Dr. P. Maas
            pointed out the parallel with the mention of <foreign>kula</foreign> ``family'' as one
            of the qualifiers for a student, in <foreign>Carakasaṃhitā</foreign> 3.8.8. </note>
    </standOff>
    <standOff corresp="#SS.1.4.7" type="Testimonia">
        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.1.4.7-n1">Parallel to the
                <title>Kātyāyanasmṛtisāroddhāraḥ</title>, Rājaguṇāḥ v.66 (Kane 1933: 11).</note>
    </standOff>
     <standOff corresp="#SS.1.6.11" type="Notes">
        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.1.6.11-n1">At this point, K adds: <foreign>(tatra
                            śaraddhemantayor madhye samam ahorātraṃ yugapac
                            cādhimāsakau bhavataḥ viṃśatikāṣṭhāś caikanimeṣārdhaś
                            ca dinaparivṛddhir uttarāyaṇe)</foreign>. The round brackets appear to
                            have been added by another hand.</note>
    </standOff>
    <standOff corresp="#SS.1.6.12" type="Notes">
        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.1.6.12-n1">In the 1931 ed. of the vulgate, the text
            numbering skips from 1.61.11 to 1.6.13, omitting the number 1.6.12. In the 1938 ed. the
            vulgate <foreign>tatra varṣāhemanta ... nirharaṇaṃ kartavyam</foreign> is numbered
            1.6.12 (not 1.6.13) and the numbers are one more than the 1931 ed. from there to the end
            of the <foreign>adhyāya</foreign>.</note>
    </standOff>
    <standOff corresp="#SS.1.10.4.1" type="Notes">
        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.1.10.4.1-n1">MS K seems to have
                <foreign>veditavyāḥ</foreign> although the final visarga isn't clear and the
            manuscript is damaged at this point. It was read as a visarga by Ācārya (1939: 77, note
            2).</note>
    </standOff>
    <standOff corresp="#SS.1.10.4.1" type="Testimonia">
        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.1.10.4.1-n2">The vulgate here adds a passage that is taken
            to be a reference to the doctrines of the <title>Carakasaṃhitā</title>. See HIML 1A,
            351-352. The commentary <title>Bhānumatī</title> by Cakrapāṇidatta does not support this
            added passage (<foreign>ayaṃ pāṭhaś cakrāsaṃmataḥ</foreign>: Ācārya 1939: 77, note 2).
        </note>
    </standOff>
    <standOff corresp="#SS.1.11.7" type="Testimonia">
        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.1.11.7-n1">Ḍalhaṇa reported that some people read
                <foreign>evānuśastrapātanam</foreign> as here, as opposed to the vulgate's
                <foreign>anuśastrapraṇidhānam</foreign>.</note>
    </standOff>
    <standOff corresp="#SS.1.13.4" type="Notes">

            an insertion at this point, although they report it differently. Ḍalhaṇa reported
            variant readings at this point in the text.</note>
    </standOff>
    <standOff corresp="#SS.1.13.5.1" type="Notes">

            this reading. </note>
    </standOff>
    <standOff corresp="#SS.1.13.7" type="Note">
        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.1.13.7-n1">The hiatus between <foreign>ca</foreign> and
                <foreign>alābu</foreign> is required for correct metre.</note>
    </standOff>
    <standOff corresp="#SS.1.13.16" type="Emendation">
        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.1.13.16-n1">The transmitted Nepalese reading
                <foreign>gṛhītvā</foreign> is hard to construe unless taken over to the start of the
            next paragraph. However, then it would sit uneasily before <foreign>atha</foreign>,
            which seems to demarcate the start of different procedural stages.</note>
    </standOff>
    <standOff corresp="#SS.1.13.18" type="Notes">
        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.1.13.18-n1">The vulgate edition notes "other readings" that
            correspond to those in the the Nepalese witnesses for <foreign>tanvyaś cākṣetrajāś ca
                yāḥ</foreign> and <foreign>poṣayet</foreign>.</note>
    </standOff>
    <standOff corresp="#SS.1.13.19" type="Notes">
        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.1.13.19-n1">Note the irregular sandhi of f. pl.
                <foreign>-sañcāriṇī</foreign>.</note>
    </standOff>
    <standOff corresp="#SS.1.13.22.1" type="Notes">

                <foreign>aprahṛṣṭaśiraḥpādakāyenodveṣṭate sakṛt | yā coṣṇaṃ kurute tāpaṃ tasyām

    <standOff corresp="#SS.1.16.7" type="Notes">
        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.1.16.7-n1">The unusual form <foreign>sthūlatarīṃ</foreign>
            is supported by both manuscripts and we have retained it in spite of only meagre
            evidence for the form in epic Sanskrit.</note>
    </standOff>
    <standOff corresp="#SS.1.16.10" type="Notes">
        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.1.16.10-n1">Cakrapāṇi (1.16.9–13) and Ḍalhaṇa (1.16.10)
            pointed out that others read <foreign>pañcadaśakarṇakṛtayaḥ</foreign> (instead of
                <foreign>pañcadaśasandhānākṛtayaḥ</foreign>). Ḍalhaṇa (1.16.10) also mentioned that
            some read <foreign>samunnatasamobhayapāliḥ</foreign> (instead of
                <foreign>vṛttāyatasamobhayapālir</foreign>) and others do not read
                <foreign>saṃkṣiptādayaḥ pañcāsādhyāḥ</foreign>.</note>
    </standOff>
    <standOff corresp="#SS.1.16.15" type="Notes">
        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.1.16.15-n1">The MSS reading <foreign>viśeṣataś
                cāgropaharaṇīyāt</foreign> has been emended to <foreign>viśeṣataś
                cātropaharet</foreign> to make sense of the list of ingredients, which is in the
            accusative case. Also, the repetition of <foreign>agropaharaṇīyāt</foreign> in the MSS
            suggests that its second occurrence, which does not make good sense here, is a
            dittographic error.</note>
    </standOff>
    <standOff corresp="#SS.1.16.15" type="Notes">
        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.1.16.15-n3">The additional verses in A (from
                <foreign>bhavanti cātra to śāstravit</foreign>) were probably also absent in the
            version of the <emph>Suśrutasaṃhitā</emph> commented on by Cakrapāṇi, who cited them in
            his commentary as being "read by some" in regard to the joins
                (<foreign>sandhāna</foreign>) they describe.</note>
    </standOff>

    <standOff corresp="#SS.1.16.19" type="Notes">
        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.1.16.19-n1">Ḍalhaṇa (1.16.18) noted that some read
                <foreign>rājasarṣapajaṃ</foreign> in the place of
            <foreign>gaurasarṣapajaṃ</foreign>. This reading appears to have been accepted by
            Cakrapāṇi (1.16.18–20), who glossed <foreign>rājasarṣapaja</foreign> as
                <foreign>śvetasarṣapa</foreign>. Cakrapāṇi also said that some read sarpis in the
            place of <foreign>payas</foreign>. In the compound beginning with
                <foreign>arka</foreign>, Ḍalhaṇa noted that some read
            <foreign>arkapuṣpī</foreign>.</note>
    </standOff>
